===\n# Blueprint Analysis: DrydockStats\n\n## Commander Expectations\n\n- assert the product reads Drydock's LLM execution log (logs/llm.jsonl) and writes six CSV reports\n- assert the product runs with the Go standard library only and no database\n- assert malformed, non-object, incomplete, and duplicate log lines are skipped without failing the run\n- assert every report matches the supplied known answers in sources/run.sh byte for byte\n- assert the Target and date filters narrow every report\n- assert the reporter is invoked as `ddkstat <report> [options] <log>`\n\n## Crew\n\n| Crew | Charge |\n|---|---|\n| Commander | Defines intent and decides what done means. |\n| Team Lead | Confirms epic completeness and stakeholder expectations. |\n| Planning Crew | Authors atomic |\n|---|---|---|\n| LOG-001 | Read JSON Lines log records from a file path | Each non-blank line is parsed as one JSON object; the file is read as UTF-8 |\n| LOG-002 | Skip non-object, incomplete, and duplicate records | Lines that are not objects, lack a required non-empty field, or repeat an earlier execution_id are skipped and counted, with a stderr warning naming the line number, and exit code is unchanged |\n| LOG-003 | Attribute each record to an executed or requested model | Model is result.stats.model when present and non-empty, otherwise job.model |\n| LOG-004 | Treat absent or null numeric stats as zero | Missing or null token counts, cost, and elapsed_ms count as zero; a record without result.stats is still a record |\n| LOG-005 | Classify a record as failed when status is not succeeded | Any status other than `succeeded` counts toward failed |\n\n### Feature: Filtering\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| FLT-001 | Filter records by Target name | Only records whose job.target equals the --target value exactly are kept |\n| FLT-002 | Filter records by start date range | --since and --until keep records whose start date (first 10 characters of started_at) is within the inclusive range |\n| FLT-003 | Validate date option values | Dates must be real calendar dates in YYYY-MM-DD form; invalid dates are a usage error (exit 2) |\n\n### Feature: Command Interface\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CLI-001 | Parse report name, options, and single log path | Report comes first, options follow, log path is last; exactly one log path is required |\n| CLI-002 | Reject usage errors with exit code 2 | Unknown report, unknown option, missing option value, invalid date, or missing/extra log path exits 2 with nothing on stdout |\n| CLI-003 | Report operational read failures with exit code 1 | A log that cannot be opened or read exits 1 with nothing on stdout and one diagnostic line on stderr |\n\n### Feature: Summary Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| SUM-001 | Produce the summary report | One CSV row with header |\n\n### Feature: Commands Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CMD-001 | Produce the commands report | One row per command ordered by calls descending then command ascending, TOTAL row, with cost and minutes columns |\n\n### Feature: Models Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| MDL-001 | Produce the models report | One row per provider and model pair start date ascending, tokens as input plus cached plus output, followed by a TOTAL row |\n\n### Feature: Failures Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| FAL-001 | Produce the failures report | One row per failed record ordered by started_at then execution_id, returncode empty when null or absent, timed_out as true or false |\n\n### Feature: Output Formatting\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| FMT-001 | Format minutes in tenths of a minute | Minutes equal (ms + 3000) / 6000 in integer division written as t/10 \".\" t%10; 95000 ms renders as 1.6 |\n| FMT-002 | Format cost with four decimal places | Summed cost_usd is written with %.4f |\n| FMT-003 | Write CSV with header, LF line endings, and TOTAL rows | Header row first, comma-separated, \\n endings, zero-match rows write header and zero TOTAL where applicable |\n\n### Feature: Build and Verification\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| BLD-001 | Provide the Go module and Criteria\n\n| ID | Story ID | Criterion |\n|---|---|---|\n| AC-001 | LOG-002 | The reporter writes exactly one stderr warning per skipped line naming its line number, and never writes warnings to skipped |\n| AC-004 | FLT-002 | A date filter that matches nothing yields a header-only (or zero TOTAL) report with exit 0 |\n| AC-005 | CLI-001 | The log path is the final argument; any argument after it, including a second log path, is a usage error |\n| AC-006 | CMD-001 | Ties in calls are ordered by command name in byte order |\n| AC-007 | FMT-002 | A zero-match cost column renders as It reads one JSON record per LLM call and answers operator questions about call counts, Targets, providers and models, tokens, minutes, cost, and failures, emitting six CSV reports. It serves Drydock operators who need usage and failure accounting without a database.\n\n## Constraints\n- Go 1.22 or newer; standard library only; no database.\n- Application root is a Go module; the executable is the main package at ./cmd/ddkstat.\n- Reports are CSV to standard output; diagnostics go to standard error only.\n- Exit codes: 0 success, 1 unreadable log, 2 usage error.\n- Do not modify sources/run.sh or sources/llm_sample.jsonl.\n\n## Guardrails\n- Never read sources/run.sh or embed a report answer in application code; every value is computed from the log.\n- Never fail the run because of a skipped line; skipping never changes the exit code.\n- Never write anything to standard output on a failing command.\n- Never compare or depend on standard error output.\n- Never use a sample answer to bypass computation.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT discovery-identity.json ===\n{\n  \"id\": \"discovery-identity\",\n  \"title\": \"Discovery: Project Identity\",\n  \"purpose\": \"Confirm the proposed display name and short description before planning.\",\n  \"questions\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": \"display_name\",\n      \"label\": \"Display Name\",\n      \"prompt\": \"The display name Drydock will use for this project. LOG-004 | Treat absent or null numeric stats as zero | Missing or null token counts, cost, and elapsed_ms count as zero; a record without result.stats is still a record |\n| LOG-005 | Classify a record as failed when status is not succeeded | Any status other than `succeeded` counts toward failed |\n\n### Feature: Filtering\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| FLT-001 | Filter records by Target name | Only records whose job.target equals the --target value exactly are kept |\n| FLT-002 | Filter records by start date range | --since and --until keep records whose start date (first 10 characters of started_at) is within the inclusive range |\n| FLT-003 | Validate date option values | Dates must be real calendar dates in YYYY-MM-DD form; invalid dates are a usage error (exit 2) |\n\n### Feature: Command Interface\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CLI-001 | Parse report name, options, and single log path | Report comes first, options follow, log path is last; exactly one log path is required |\n| CLI-002 | Reject usage errors with exit code 2 | Unknown report, unknown option, missing option value, invalid date, or missing/extra log path exits 2 with nothing on stdout |\n| CLI-003 | Report operational read failures with exit code 1 | A log that cannot be opened or read exits 1 with nothing on stdout and one diagnostic line on stderr |\n\n### Feature: Summary Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| SUM-001 | Produce the summary report | One CSV row with header records,skipped,succeeded,failed,first,last,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,cost_usd,minutes |\n| SUM-002 | Count skipped lines over the whole file | The skipped count ignores filters |\n\n### Feature: Commands Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| CMD-001 | Produce the commands report | One row per command ordered by calls descending then command ascending, followed by a TOTAL row |\n\n### Feature: Targets Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| TGT-001 | Produce the targets report | One row per Target ordered ascending, followed by a TOTAL row, with cost and minutes columns |\n\n### Feature: Models Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| MDL-001 | Produce the models report | One row per provider and model pair ordered by provider then model ascending, followed by a TOTAL row |\n\n### Feature: Daily Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| DLY-001 | Produce the daily report | One row per start date ascending, tokens as input plus cached plus output, followed by a TOTAL row |\n\n### Feature: Failures Report\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| FAL-001 | Produce the failures report | One row per failed record ordered by started_at then execution_id, returncode empty when null or absent, timed_out as true or false |\n\n### Feature: Output Formatting\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| FMT-001 | Format minutes in tenths of a minute | Minutes equal (ms + 3000) / 6000 in integer division written as t/10 \".\" t%10; 95000 ms renders as 1.6 |\n| FMT-002 | Format cost with four decimal places | Summed cost_usd is written with %.4f |\n| FMT-003 | Write CSV with header, LF line endings, and TOTAL rows | Header row first, comma-separated, \\n endings, zero-match rows write header and zero TOTAL where applicable |\n\n### Feature: Build and Verification\n\n| ID | Story | High-level AC |\n|---|---|---|\n| BLD-001 | Provide the Go module and ddkstat entry point | Go module root with main package at ./cmd/ddkstat, standard library only, builds with go build |\n| BLD-002 | Provide bin/test.sh over vet and tests | bin/test.sh is POSIX sh, exits zero only when go vet ./... and go test ./... both pass |\n| BLD-003 | Pass the supplied grader | sh sources/run.sh exits zero with all 20 checks passing |\n\n## Surfaced Acceptance Criteria\n\n| ID | Story ID | Criterion |\n|---|---|---|\n| AC-001 | LOG-002 | The reporter writes exactly one stderr warning per skipped line naming its line number, and never writes warnings to stdout |\n| AC-002 | LOG-002 | A duplicate execution_id is skipped only on its later occurrences; the first occurrence is kept |\n| AC-003 | LOG-001 | Blank lines are ignored and not counted as DLY-001 | Dates are ordered ascending by byte value of the YYYY-MM-DD string |\n\n## Relationship Model\n\n| Source or group | Relationship type | Related source or group | Evidence | Delivery | context | stage |\n\n## Planning Instructions\n\n### Delivery Shape\n\nThe system is a single Go command-line executable. Input: one JSON Lines file path plus optional `--target`, `--since`, and `--until` filters. Output: one CSV report to standard output, with diagnostics on standard error. Required execution flow: parse arguments, open and read the log, skip invalid lines, apply filters, aggregate, and write one of six reports.\n\n### Story Realization Map\n\n- LOG-001 to LOG-005: `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` Rules 1–5 and the Log section; exercised by `sources/llm_sample.jsonl`.\n- FLT-001 to FLT-003: `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` Command and Rule 6; date validation per Exit codes section.\n- CLI-001 to CLI-003: `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` Command and Exit codes table.\n- SUM-001 to SUM-002: `sources/INSTRUCTIONS.md` Output table row `summary`; `sources/run.sh` A1, B1, C2.\n- CMD-001: Output table row `commands`; `sources/run.sh` A2, B2, C1.\n- TGT-001: Output table row `targets`; `sources/run.sh` A3.\n- MDL-001: Output table row `models`; `sources/run.sh` A4, B5.\n- DLY-001: Output table row `daily`; `sources/run.sh` A5, B3.\n- FAL-001: Output table row `failures`; `sources/run.sh` A6, B4.\n- FMT-001 to FMT-003: Rules 7–9 and Output section.\n- BLD-001 to BLD-003: Stack section, Tests section, and `sources/run.sh` build step.\n\n### Test and Acceptance Strategy\n\nFocused story tests cover each report, filter, skip rule, and exit code using logs written by the tests (`bin/test.sh`). Final verification is the single terminal story BLD-003, which runs the unfiltered grader `sh sources/run.sh`. That story declares `Suite: full`. Other stories run only their owned slice and declare `Suite: scoped`.\n\n### Sequencing and Dependencies\n\nBuild BLD-001 first. LOG-001 and LOG-002 precede every report and filter story. LOG-003 through LOG-005 and FLT-* precede report stories. FMT-* precede the report stories that use them. BLD-002 follows all implementation stories. BLD-003 is terminal and depends on every implementation story.\n\n### Source Conflicts and Gaps\n\nNone found. It reads one JSON record per LLM call and answers operator questions about call counts, Targets, providers and models, tokens, minutes, cost, and failures, emitting six CSV reports. It serves Drydock operators who need usage and failure accounting without a database.\n\n## Constraints\n- Go 1.22 or newer; standard library only; no database.\n- Application root is a Go module; the executable is the main package at ./cmd/ddkstat.\n- Reports are CSV to standard output; diagnostics go to standard error only.\n- Exit codes: 0 success, 1 unreadable log, 2 usage error.\n- Do not modify sources/run.sh or sources/llm_sample.jsonl.\n\n## Guardrails\n- Never read sources/run.sh or embed a report answer in application code; every value is computed from the log.\n- Never fail the run because of a skipped line; skipping never changes the exit code.\n- Never write anything to standard output on a failing command.\n- Never compare or depend on standard error output.\n- Never use a sample answer to bypass computation.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT discovery-identity.json ===\n{\n  \"id\": \"discovery-identity\",\n  \"title\": \"Discovery: Project Identity\",\n  \"purpose\": \"Confirm the proposed display name and short description before planning.\",\n  \"questions\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": \"display_name\",\n      \"label\": \"Display Name\",\n      \"prompt\": \"The display name Drydock will use for this project.
